Option Explicit
Public T_OTOSP1E,T_OTOSP1T
Public Webden, GecersizKodlar(), PasGecilenTarihler(), SonPazartesi
dim pe1, ustmargin, solMargin, sagMargin, altMargin, satirYuksekligi, satirPozr
'öncelikle bu rutin çaðrýlýyor....
'/*main*/
Sub Makro1()
	Dim Uid
	Dim Password, Basla, objxmlDoc, URL, URL2, k,z,Ek,dosya	

	Set T_OTOSP1E=Doc.GetTableObject("OTOSP1E")
	Set T_OTOSP1T=Doc.GetTableObject("OTOSP1T")	
	ReDim GecersizKodlar(0)
	ReDim PasGecilenTarihler(0)
	Webden = 1
	if Webden = 1 then
		Uid=Trim(T_OTOSP1E.UID)
		Password=Trim(T_OTOSP1E.PASSWORD)
		if Uid="" or Password="" then
			'//30.7.2012
			'//Doc.d7MsgBox "Kullanýcý Adý veya Parola Boþ Olamaz. Ýþlem Ýptal.","ERP",MB_OK
			Doc.d7MsgBox Doc.GetMyMessage(3624),"ERP",MB_OK
			Exit Sub
		end if
	end if
	T_OTOSP1T.Empty
	Set objXMLDoc = CreateObject("Microsoft.XMLDOM")
	objXMLDoc.async = False
	if Webden = 1 then

		Dim CookieStr
		'//1.8.2012
		'//Doc.StatusBarMessage "FOSN 'e baðlanýlýyor"
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "FOSN " & Doc.GetMyMessage(4103)
		CookieStr = FOSNConnect(Uid, Password)
		If Trim(CookieStr) = "" Then
			'//30.7.2012
			'//k = Doc.d7MsgBox("FOSN 'e baðlanýlamadý. Ýþlem Kesilmiþtir", "Hata!", MB_OK)
			k = Doc.d7MsgBox(Doc.GetMyMessage(3625), Doc.GetMyMessage(584), MB_OK)
			Exit Sub
		End if

		Dim GunlukPrgxml
		'//1.8.2012
		'//Doc.StatusBarMessage "Günlük program alýnýyor"
		Doc.StatusBarMessage Doc.GetMyMessage(4104)
		Set GunlukPrgxml = GetPrgXML(cookieStr, "G")

		Dim HaftalikPrgxml
		'//1.8.2012
		'//Doc.StatusBarMessage "Haftalýk program alýnýyor"
		Doc.StatusBarMessage Doc.GetMyMessage(4105)
		Set HaftalikPrgxml = GetPrgXML(cookieStr, "H")

		objXMLDoc.Loadxml GunlukPrgxml.xml
	else
		dosya = "c:\FO_GUNLUKP.xml"
		objXMLDoc.Load dosya
	end if

'	Dim Fso, ProgramFile
'	Set Fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
'	Set ProgramFile = Fso.CreateTextFile("c:\gunlukp.xml",True)
'	ProgramFile.WriteLine objXMLDoc.xml
'	ProgramFile.Close
'	exit sub

	if trim(objXMLDoc.xml) = "" then
		'//30.7.2012
		'//z = Doc.D7MsgBox("Günlük program alýnamadý." & vbcrlf & "Ýþlem kesilmiþtir.","ERP",MB_OK)
		z = Doc.D7MsgBox(Doc.GetMyMessage(3626) & vbcrlf & Doc.GetMyMessage(319),"ERP",MB_OK)
		Doc.StatusBarMessage ""
		exit Sub
	end if
	objXMLDoc.LoadXML Ek & vbcrlf & objXMLDoc.xml
	
	Dim i,y, Root,NodeList, ST40, kk, Attr, Schedule, Qty, TesTarih, SatirNo, yy, Sc, ProgramList

	y = 1
	Set Root = objXMLDoc.documentElement
	Set ProgramList = Root.getElementsByTagName("PROGRAM")

	'//1.8.2012
	'//Doc.StatusBarMessage "Aktarýlýyor"
	Doc.StatusBarMessage Doc.GetMyMessage(4106)

	Doc.CreateTableObject "XXX=OTOSP1L","TMPCOPY"
	Set ST40 = Doc.GetTableObject("XXX")

	Dim ST40T1
	Doc.CreateTableObject "YYY=OTOSP1L","TMPCOPY"
	Set ST40T1 = Doc.GetTableObject("YYY")	

	Dim ST40T2
	Doc.CreateTableObject "ZZZ=OTOSP1L","TMPCOPY"
	Set ST40T2 = Doc.GetTableObject("ZZZ")	

	Dim ProgramNo, FabrikaKodu, SevkEdilenFabrikaKodu, AraSevk, Program, SonASNNo, SonASNTarih, SonASNMiktar, SonASNKumule
	Dim DepoLokasyon, GolcukLokasyon
	kk = ElemanSayisi(objXMLDoc)
	For each Program in ProgramList
		Set ProgramNo = Program.SelectSingleNode("PROGRAM_NO")
		Set FabrikaKodu = Program.SelectSingleNode("PLANT_CODE")
		Set SevkEdilenFabrikaKodu = Program.SelectSingleNode("SHIP_TO_PLANT_CODE")
		Set AraSevk = Program.SelectSingleNode("SHIP_FROM_PLANT_CODE")

		Set NodeList = Program.GetElementsByTagName("PART")

		For i = 0 To NodeList.Length - 1
			Set Attr = NodeList.Item(i).Attributes
			Set Schedule = NodeList.Item(i).GetElementsByTagName("SCHEDULE_ITEM")
			Set SonASNNo = NodeList.Item(i).SelectSingleNode("LAST_ASN_NO")
			Set SonASNTarih = NodeList.Item(i).SelectSingleNode("LAST_ASN_DATE")
			Set SonASNMiktar = NodeList.Item(i).SelectSingleNode("LAST_ASN_NET_QUANTITY")
			Set SonASNKumule = NodeList.Item(i).SelectSingleNode("LAST_ASN_CUM_QUANTITY")
			Set DepoLokasyon = NodeList.Item(i).SelectSingleNode("CD_STORAGE_LOC")

			For Each Sc In Schedule
				Set Qty = Sc.SelectSingleNode("NET_QUANTITY")
				Set TesTarih = Sc.SelectSingleNode("DATE")
				If Qty.Text <> "0" Then 
					If y <> 1 Then
						k = ST40.AddRow()
						k = ST40T1.AddRow()
						k = ST40T2.AddRow()
					Else
						If ST40.GetRecCount = 0 Then
							k = ST40.AddRow()
							k = ST40T1.AddRow()
							k = ST40T2.AddRow()
						End If
					End If
					y = 2
					if not DepoLokasyon is Nothing Then
						GolcukLokasyon = DepoLokasyon.Text
'						if trim(GolcukLokasyon) <> "" Then
'							msgbox GolcukLokasyon
'							exit sub
'						end if
					Else
						GolcukLokasyon = ""
					End if
					SatirNo = ST40.GetRecCount()
					ST40.KOD(satirNo) = Temizle(Attr.Item(0).Text)
					ST40.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo) = Doc.CDbl(Qty.Text)
					ST40.RTESTARIH(satirNo) = FmtTarih(TesTarih.Text)
					ST40.PROGRAM_NO(satirNo) = ProgramNo.Text
					ST40.FABRIKA_KODU(satirNo) = FabrikaKodu.Text
					ST40.SEVK_EDILEN_FB(satirNo) = SevkEdilenFabrikaKodu.Text
					ST40.ARA_SEVK(satirNo) = AraSevk.Text
					ST40.ASN_NO(satirNo) = Right(GetIrsNo(SonASNNo.Text),10)
					ST40.ASN_TARIHI(satirNo) = FmtTarih(SonASNTarih.Text)
					ST40.ASN_MIKTARI(satirNo) = Doc.Cdbl(SonASNMiktar.Text)
					ST40.ASN_MIKTARI_KUMULE(satirNo) = Doc.Cdbl(SonASNKumule.Text)
					ST40.LOKASYON(satirNo) = GolcukLokasyon

					ST40T1.KOD(satirNo) = Temizle(Attr.Item(0).Text)
					ST40T1.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo) = Qty.Text
					ST40T1.RTESTARIH(satirNo) = FmtTarih(TesTarih.Text)
					ST40T1.ASN_NO(satirNo) = Right(GetIrsNo(SonASNNo.Text),10)
					ST40T1.ASN_TARIHI(satirNo) = FmtTarih(SonASNTarih.Text)
					ST40T1.ASN_MIKTARI(satirNo) = Doc.Cdbl(SonASNMiktar.Text)
					ST40T1.ASN_MIKTARI_KUMULE(satirNo) = Doc.Cdbl(SonASNKumule.Text)
					ST40T1.LOKASYON(satirNo) = GolcukLokasyon

					ST40T2.KOD(satirNo) = Temizle(Attr.Item(0).Text)
					ST40T2.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo) = Doc.CDbl(Qty.Text) * (-1)
					ST40T2.RTESTARIH(satirNo) = Pazartesi(FmtTarih(TesTarih.Text))
					ST40T2.PROGRAM_NO(satirNo) = ProgramNo.Text
					ST40T2.FABRIKA_KODU(satirNo) = FabrikaKodu.Text
					ST40T2.SEVK_EDILEN_FB(satirNo) = SevkEdilenFabrikaKodu.Text
					ST40T2.ARA_SEVK(satirNo) = AraSevk.Text
					ST40T2.ATTRIB(satirNo) = "W"
					ST40T2.ASN_NO(satirNo) = Right(GetIrsNo(SonASNNo.Text),10)
					ST40T2.ASN_TARIHI(satirNo) = FmtTarih(SonASNTarih.Text)
					ST40T2.ASN_MIKTARI(satirNo) = Doc.Cdbl(SonASNMiktar.Text)
					ST40T2.ASN_MIKTARI_KUMULE(satirNo) = Doc.Cdbl(SonASNKumule.Text)
					ST40T2.LOKASYON(satirNo) = GolcukLokasyon
					yy = yy + 1 
					'//1.8.2012
					'//Doc.StatusBarMessage "XML okunuyor : " & yy & " / " & kk
					Doc.StatusBarMessage Doc.GetMyMessage(4107) & " : " & yy & " / " & kk
				End If
			Next
		Next
	Next
	Dim Btarih, STarih,Fark,Ptesi, stSatirSayisi, sonKolon
	BTarih = IlkTarih(ST40)

	Starih = Sontarih(ST40)
	Fark = 8 - Weekday(Starih, 2)
	Ptesi = replace(Doc.StrDate_Increment(Starih, Fark),"/","")

'***********
	if ST40T1.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		Dim Tarih, SonGun
		ST40T1.GroupBy("RTESTARIH")
		ST40T1.Sort("RTESTARIH")
		SonGun = ST40T1.RTESTARIH(ST40T1.GetRecCount)
		sonPazartesi = Pazartesi(SonGun)
		T_OTOSP1T.AddRow
		for i = 1 to ST40T1.GetRecCount()
			Tarih = ST40T1.RTESTARIH(i)
			res = Doc.SetFieldValue("OTOSP1T","TARIH" & i,1,Tarih)	
			res = Doc.SetFieldValue("OTOSP1T","ATTRIB" & i,1,"G")				
		Next
	End if
'***********

	ST40T2.GroupBy("PROGRAM_NO+FABRIKA_KODU+SEVK_EDILEN_FB+ARA_SEVK+KOD+RTESTARIH")
	ST40.GroupBy("PROGRAM_NO+FABRIKA_KODU+SEVK_EDILEN_FB+ARA_SEVK+KOD+RTESTARIH")
	ST40.Sort("PROGRAM_NO+FABRIKA_KODU+SEVK_EDILEN_FB+ARA_SEVK+KOD+RTESTARIH")


	Dim rep,sKol, Miktar, res, KodVar, StokKodu,MstokKodu, Birim
	stSatirSayisi = ST40.GetRecCount()
	SonKolon = 1
	For i = 1 To stSatirSayisi
		'//1.8.2012
		'//Doc.StatusBarMessage "Yazýlýyor : " & i & " / " & stSatirSayisi
		Doc.StatusBarMessage Doc.GetMyMessage(4108) & " : " & i & " / " & stSatirSayisi
		StokKodu = ST40.KOD(i)
		Birim = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","KOD", StokKodu,"")
		KodVar = 0
		if trim(Birim) = "" then
			MStokKodu = Doc.Get0G0U(StokKodu,T_OTOSP1E.CH_KODU,"12","")
			if trim(MStokKodu) = "" or trim(MStokKodu) = trim(StokKodu) then
				KodVar = 0
			else
				KodVar = 1
			end if
		else
			KodVar = 1
		end if
		if KodVar = 1 then
			If T_OTOSP1T.GetRecCount = 0 Then
				T_OTOSP1T.AddRow
				satirNo = T_OTOSP1T.GetRecCount
			ElseIf T_OTOSP1T.GetRecCount = 1 And trim(T_OTOSP1T.KOD(1)) = "" Then
				satirNo = 1
			Else
				satirNo = satirBul(i,ST40)
				if satirNo = 0 then
					T_OTOSP1T.AddRow
					satirNo = T_OTOSP1T.GetRecCount()
				end if
				T_OTOSP1T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
			End If
			T_OTOSP1T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
			T_OTOSP1T.D_ID(satirNo) = ST40.KOD(i)
			T_OTOSP1T.PROGRAM_NO(satirNo) = ST40.PROGRAM_NO(i)
			T_OTOSP1T.FABRIKA_KODU(satirNo) = ST40.FABRIKA_KODU(i)
			T_OTOSP1T.SEVK_EDILEN_FB(satirNo) = ST40.SEVK_EDILEN_FB(i)
			T_OTOSP1T.ARA_SEVK(satirNo) = ST40.ARA_SEVK(i)
			T_OTOSP1T.ASN_NO(satirNo) = ST40.ASN_NO(i)
			T_OTOSP1T.ASN_TARIHI(satirNo) = ST40.ASN_TARIHI(i)
			T_OTOSP1T.ASN_MIKTARI(satirNo) = ST40.ASN_MIKTARI(i)
			T_OTOSP1T.ASN_MIKTARI_KUMULE(satirNo) = ST40.ASN_MIKTARI_KUMULE(i)
			T_OTOSP1T.LOKASYON(satirNo) = ST40.LOKASYON(i)

			Tarih = ST40.RTESTARIH(i)
			Rep = AlanBul1(Tarih,"G")
			if Tarih > STarih then STarih = Tarih
			Miktar = ST40.SF_MIKTAR(i)
			If Trim(Rep)="" then
				'//21.09.2012 11:49:41
				'//PasGecilenTarihler(uBound(PasGecilenTarihler)) = "[ Günlük ]-" & "[" & Tarih & "]"
				PasGecilenTarihler(uBound(PasGecilenTarihler)) = "[ " & Doc.GetMyMessage(5446) & " ]-" & "[" & Tarih & "]"

				Redim Preserve PasGecilenTarihler(uBound(PasGecilenTarihler)+1)				
			else
				res = Doc.SetFieldValue("OTOSP1T",Rep,satirNo,Miktar + T_OTOSP1T.GetFieldvalue(Rep,satirNo))
			end if			
		else
			if GecersizBul(StokKodu) = 0 then
				GecersizKodlar(uBound(GecersizKodlar)) = StokKodu
				Redim Preserve GecersizKodlar(uBound(GecersizKodlar)+1)
			end if
		end if
	Next
	'//21.09.2012 11:50:10
	'//T_OTOSP1E.ACIKLAMA = "Baþlangýç tarihi : " & Doc.StrToNordate(BTarih)
	T_OTOSP1E.ACIKLAMA = Doc.GetMyMessage(5447) & " : " & Doc.StrToNordate(BTarih)

	dim bitis, sCol, j, BaslangicKolon, xAttrib

	for j = 2 to T_OTOSP1T.GetRecCount
		For i = 1 to 40
			xAttrib = T_OTOSP1T.GetFieldValue("ATTRIB" & i,1)
			if xAttrib = "G" then
				Tarih = T_OTOSP1T.GetFieldValue("TARIH" & i,1)
				res = Doc.SetFieldValue("OTOSP1T","TARIH" & i,j,Tarih)	
				res = Doc.SetFieldValue("OTOSP1T","ATTRIB" & i,j,"G")	
			end if
		Next
	Next

	Set ST40 = Nothing
	Set ObjXMLDoc = Nothing

	Call HaftalikPlan(ST40T2, HaftalikPrgxml)

	Call Tamamla()

	Call ToplamHesapla()

	Doc.RunCommand("CMD_SETCAPTIONS")

	Doc.RunCommand("CMD_STOK_VE_MPS_HESAPLA_FROM_SCRIPTS")

	'//1.8.2012
	'//Doc.StatusBarMessage "FOSN baðlantýsý kesiliyor..."
	Doc.StatusBarMessage "FOSN " & Doc.GetMyMessage(4109)
	'Call FOSNLogOut()

	dim ret, Gecersiz, Gecersizler, PasGecilen, GecersizKod
	PasGecilen = False
	GecersizKod = False

	if isArray(PasGecilenTarihler) then
		if trim(PasGecilenTarihler(0)) <> "" Then
			PasGecilen = True
		End if 
	End if
	if isArray(GecersizKodlar) then
		if trim(GecersizKodlar(0)) <> "" Then
			GecersizKod = True
		End if 
	End if

	if PasGecilen or GecersizKod Then
		Call vpe_ayarlari()

		if PasGecilen then
			'//21.09.2012 11:50:49
			'//Call VPEYaz(PasGecilenTarihler,"Pas Geçilen Tarihler")
			Call VPEYaz(PasGecilenTarihler,Doc.GetMyMessage(5448))
		end if

		if GecersizKod then
			'//21.09.2012 11:51:06
			'//Call VPEYaz(GecersizKodlar,"Geçersiz Kodlar :")
			Call VPEYaz(GecersizKodlar,Doc.GetMyMessage(5449) & " :")
		end if
		Call vpe_son()
	End if

	dim son
	Doc.RunCommand "CMD_TABLOSIPARISTEN_AL"
	'//30.7.2012
	'//son = doc.d7msgbox("Ýþlem Tamamlandý","ERP",MB_OK)
	son = Doc.d7msgbox(Doc.GetMyMessage(917),"ERP",MB_OK)
End Sub
Sub HaftalikPlan(ST40T2, HaftalikPrgxml)
	Dim Basla, objXMLDoc, k, Ek, z


	Set objXMLDoc = CreateObject("Microsoft.XMLDOM")
	objXMLDoc.async = False

	Ek = "<?xml version=" & chr(34) & "1.0" & chr(34) & " encoding=" & chr(34) & "ISO-8859-9" & chr(34) &"?>"

	dim dosya
	'//1.8.2012
	'//Doc.StatusBarMessage "Haftalýk program alýnýyor"
	Doc.StatusBarMessage Doc.GetMyMessage(4105)
	if Webden = 1 then
		objXMLDoc.LoadXML HaftalikPrgxml.xml
	else
		dosya = "C:\FO_HAFTALIKP.xml"
		objXMLDoc.Load dosya
	end if
	Dim y, Root,NodeList,ST40,kk,i,Attr,Schedule, Sc,ForecastType, Qty, TesTarih,satirNo, yy, ProgramList

	y = 1
	Set Root = objXMLDoc.documentElement
	Set ProgramList = Root.getElementsByTagName("PROGRAM")

	'//1.8.2012
	'//Doc.StatusBarMessage "Aktarýlýyor"
	Doc.StatusBarMessage Doc.GetMyMessage(4106)

	Doc.CreateTableObject "XXX=OTOSP1L","TMPCOPY"
	Set ST40 = Doc.GetTableObject("XXX")
	
	Dim ST40T1
	Doc.CreateTableObject "YYY=OTOSP1L","TMPCOPY"
	Set ST40T1 = Doc.GetTableObject("YYY")	

	Dim xAttrib
	kk = ElemanSayisiHafta(objXMLDoc)

	Dim ProgramNo, FabrikaKodu, SevkEdilenFabrikaKodu, AraSevk, Program, SonASNNo, SonASNTarih, SonASNMiktar, SonASNKumule
	Dim DepoLokasyon, GolcukLokasyon

	For each Program in ProgramList
		Set ProgramNo = Program.SelectSingleNode("PROGRAM_NO")
		Set FabrikaKodu = Program.SelectSingleNode("PLANT_CODE")
		Set SevkEdilenFabrikaKodu = Program.SelectSingleNode("SHIP_TO_PLANT_CODE")
		Set AraSevk = Program.SelectSingleNode("SHIP_FROM_PLANT_CODE")

		Set NodeList = Program.getElementsByTagName("PART")

		For i = 0 To NodeList.Length - 1
			Set Attr = NodeList.Item(i).Attributes
			Set Schedule = NodeList.Item(i).getElementsByTagName("FORECAST_ITEM")
			Set SonASNNo = NodeList.Item(i).SelectSingleNode("LAST_ASN_NO")
			Set SonASNTarih = NodeList.Item(i).SelectSingleNode("LAST_ASN_DATE")
			Set SonASNMiktar = NodeList.Item(i).SelectSingleNode("LAST_ASN_NET_QUANTITY")
			Set SonASNKumule = NodeList.Item(i).SelectSingleNode("LAST_ASN_CUM_QUANTITY")
			Set DepoLokasyon = NodeList.Item(i).SelectSingleNode("STORAGE_LOCATION")

			For Each Sc In Schedule
				ForecastType = Sc.Attributes.Item(0).Text
				Set Qty = Sc.SelectSingleNode("NET_QUANTITY")
				Set TesTarih = Sc.SelectSingleNode("PERIOD_START_DATE")
'			If Qty.Text <> "0" and ForecastType = "W" and TesTarih.Text >= Replace(BaslangicTarih,"/","") Then 
				If Qty.Text <> "0" and FmtTarih(TesTarih.Text) >= 	sonPazartesi Then 
					If y <> 1 Then
						k = ST40.AddRow()
					Else
						If ST40.GetRecCount = 0 Then
							k = ST40.AddRow()
						End If
					End If
					y = 2
					SatirNo = ST40.GetRecCount()
					if not DepoLokasyon is Nothing Then
						GolcukLokasyon = DepoLokasyon.Text
					Else
						GolcukLokasyon = ""
					End if

					ST40.KOD(satirNo) = Temizle(Attr.Item(0).Text)
					ST40.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo) = Qty.Text
					ST40.RTESTARIH(satirNo) = FmtTarih(TesTarih.Text)
					ST40.ATTRIB(satirNo) = ForecastType
					ST40.PROGRAM_NO(satirNo) = ProgramNo.Text
					ST40.FABRIKA_KODU(satirNo) = FabrikaKodu.Text
					ST40.SEVK_EDILEN_FB(satirNo) = SevkEdilenFabrikaKodu.Text
					ST40.ARA_SEVK(satirNo) = AraSevk.Text
					ST40.ASN_NO(satirNo) = Right(GetIrsNo(SonASNNo.Text),10)
					ST40.ASN_TARIHI(satirNo) = FmtTarih(SonASNTarih.Text)
					ST40.ASN_MIKTARI(satirNo) = Doc.Cdbl(SonASNMiktar.Text)
					ST40.ASN_MIKTARI_KUMULE(satirNo) = Doc.Cdbl(SonASNKumule.Text)
					ST40.LOKASYON(satirNo) = GolcukLokasyon

					yy = yy + 1 
					'//1.8.2012
					'//Doc.StatusBarMessage "XML okunuyor : " & yy & " / " & kk
					Doc.StatusBarMessage "XML " & Doc.GetMyMessage(2056) & " : " & yy & " / " & kk
				End If
			Next
		Next
	Next
	for i = 1 To ST40T2.GetRecCount()
		if ST40T2.RTESTARIH(i) = SonPazartesi then
			ST40.AddRow
			SatirNo = ST40.GetRecCount()
			ST40.KOD(satirNo) = ST40T2.KOD(i)
			ST40.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo) = ST40T2.SF_MIKTAR(i)
			ST40.RTESTARIH(satirNo) = ST40T2.RTESTARIH(i)
			ST40.ATTRIB(satirNo) = ST40T2.ATTRIB(i)
			ST40.PROGRAM_NO(satirNo) = ST40T2.PROGRAM_NO(i)
			ST40.FABRIKA_KODU(satirNo) = ST40T2.FABRIKA_KODU(i)
			ST40.SEVK_EDILEN_FB(satirNo) = ST40T2.SEVK_EDILEN_FB(i)
			ST40.ARA_SEVK(satirNo) = ST40T2.ARA_SEVK(i)
			ST40.ASN_NO(satirNo) = ST40T2.ASN_NO(i)
			ST40.ASN_TARIHI(satirNo) = ST40T2.ASN_TARIHI(i)
			ST40.ASN_MIKTARI(satirNo) = ST40T2.ASN_MIKTARI(i)
			ST40.ASN_MIKTARI_KUMULE(satirNo) = ST40T2.ASN_MIKTARI_KUMULE(i)
			ST40.LOKASYON(satirNo) = ST40T2.LOKASYON(i)
		end if
	Next
	for i = 1 to ST40.GetRecCount()
		ST40T1.AddRow
		satirNo = ST40T1.GetRecCount()
		ST40T1.KOD(satirNo) = ST40.KOD(i)
		ST40T1.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo) = ST40.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		ST40T1.RTESTARIH(satirNo) = ST40.RTESTARIH(i)
		ST40T1.ATTRIB(satirNo) = ST40.ATTRIB(i)
		ST40T1.ASN_NO(satirNo) = ST40.ASN_NO(i)
		ST40T1.ASN_TARIHI(satirNo) = ST40.ASN_TARIHI(i)
		ST40T1.ASN_MIKTARI(satirNo) = ST40.ASN_MIKTARI(i)
		ST40T1.ASN_MIKTARI_KUMULE(satirNo) = ST40.ASN_MIKTARI_KUMULE(i)
		ST40T1.LOKASYON(satirNo) = ST40.LOKASYON(i)
	Next
	Dim BaslangicKolon
	for i = 1 to 40
		xAttrib = T_OTOSP1T.GetFieldValue("ATTRIB" & i,1)
		if trim(xAttrib) = "" then
			BaslangicKolon = i
			exit for
		end if
	Next
'***********
	Dim Tarih, Kolon
	ST40T1.GroupBy("ATTRIB+RTESTARIH")
	ST40T1.Sort("<ATTRIB+RTESTARIH")
	if T_OTOSP1T.GetRecCount() = 0 then
		T_OTOSP1T.AddRow
	end if
	for i = 1 to ST40T1.GetRecCount()
		Tarih = ST40T1.RTESTARIH(i)
		xAttrib = ST40T1.ATTRIB(i)
		if xAttrib = "W" then
			xAttrib = "H"
		elseif xAttrib = "F" then
			xAttrib = "A"
		end if
		Kolon = BaslangicKolon + i - 1
		res = Doc.SetFieldValue("OTOSP1T","TARIH" & Kolon,1,Tarih)	
		res = Doc.SetFieldValue("OTOSP1T","ATTRIB" & Kolon,1,xAttrib)				
	Next
'***********

	ST40.GroupBy("PROGRAM_NO+FABRIKA_KODU+SEVK_EDILEN_FB+ARA_SEVK+KOD+ATTRIB+RTESTARIH")
	ST40.Sort("PROGRAM_NO+FABRIKA_KODU+SEVK_EDILEN_FB+ARA_SEVK+KOD+<ATTRIB+RTESTARIH")

	dim stSatirSayisi, StokKodu,satir,Rep,sKol,Miktar, res, SonKolon, MStokKodu, Birim, KodVar
	stSatirSayisi = ST40.GetRecCount()
	For i = 1 To stSatirSayisi
		'//1.8.2012
		'//Doc.StatusBarMessage "Yazýlýyor : " & i & " / " & stSatirSayisi
		Doc.StatusBarMessage Doc.GetMyMessage(4108) & " : " & i & " / " & stSatirSayisi
		if ST40.SF_MIKTAR(i) > 0 then
			StokKodu = ST40.KOD(i)
			Birim = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","KOD",StokKodu,"")
			if trim(Birim) = "" then
				MStokKodu = Doc.Get0G0U(StokKodu,T_OTOSP1E.CH_KODU,"12","")
				if trim(MStokKodu) = "" or trim(MStokKodu) = trim(StokKodu) then
					KodVar = 0
				else
					KodVar = 1
				end if
			else
				KodVar = 1
			end if
			if KodVar = 1 then
				satirNo = satirBul(i,ST40)
				if satirNo = 0 then
					T_OTOSP1T.AddRow
					satirNo = T_OTOSP1T.GetRecCount
					T_OTOSP1T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
					T_OTOSP1T.D_ID(satirNo) = ST40.KOD(i)
					T_OTOSP1T.PROGRAM_NO(satirNo) = ST40.PROGRAM_NO(i)
					T_OTOSP1T.FABRIKA_KODU(satirNo) = ST40.FABRIKA_KODU(i)
					T_OTOSP1T.SEVK_EDILEN_FB(satirNo) = ST40.SEVK_EDILEN_FB(i)
					T_OTOSP1T.ARA_SEVK(satirNo) = ST40.ARA_SEVK(i)
					T_OTOSP1T.ASN_NO(satirNo) = ST40.ASN_NO(i)
					T_OTOSP1T.ASN_TARIHI(satirNo) = ST40.ASN_TARIHI(i)
					T_OTOSP1T.ASN_MIKTARI(satirNo) = ST40.ASN_MIKTARI(i)
					T_OTOSP1T.ASN_MIKTARI_KUMULE(satirNo) = ST40.ASN_MIKTARI_KUMULE(i)
					T_OTOSP1T.LOKASYON(satirNo) = ST40.LOKASYON(i)
				End If
				Tarih = ST40.RTESTARIH(i)
				xAttrib = ST40.ATTRIB(i)
				Rep = AlanBul1(Tarih,xAttrib)
				Miktar = ST40.SF_MIKTAR(i)
				If Trim(Rep)="" then				
					'Doc.d7MsgBox Doc.GetMyMessage(1244) & " [" & Tarih & "] Attrib [" & xAttrib & "] Alan [" & Rep & "] Index [" & satirNo & "]","ERP",MB_OK
					if xAttrib = "H" then
						PasGecilenTarihler(uBound(PasGecilenTarihler)) = "[ " & Doc.GetMyMessage(3627) & " ]-" & "[" & Tarih & "]"
					Elseif xAttrib = "A" then
						PasGecilenTarihler(uBound(PasGecilenTarihler)) = "[ " & Doc.GetMyMessage(3628) & " ]-" & "[" & Tarih & "]"
					end if					
					Redim Preserve PasGecilenTarihler(uBound(PasGecilenTarihler)+1)				
				else
					res = Doc.SetFieldValue("OTOSP1T",Rep,satirNo,Miktar + T_OTOSP1T.GetFieldvalue(Rep,satirNo))
				end if
			else
				if GecersizBul(StokKodu) = 0 then
					GecersizKodlar(uBound(GecersizKodlar)) = StokKodu
					Redim Preserve GecersizKodlar(uBound(GecersizKodlar)+1)
				end if
			end if
		end if
	Next

	Dim j
	for j = 1 to T_OTOSP1T.GetRecCount
		For i = BaslangicKolon to 40
			Tarih = T_OTOSP1T.GetFieldValue("TARIH" & i,1)
			xAttrib = T_OTOSP1T.GetFieldValue("ATTRIB" & i,1)
			res = Doc.SetFieldValue("OTOSP1T","TARIH" & i,j,Tarih)	
			res = Doc.SetFieldValue("OTOSP1T","ATTRIB" & i,j,xAttrib)	
		Next
	Next

	Set ST40 = Nothing
	Set ST40T1 = Nothing
End Sub
Sub Tamamla()
	Dim j,i, OncekiTarih, OncekiAttrib, res
	for j = 1 to 40
		if trim(T_OTOSP1T.GetFieldValue("TARIH" & j,1)) <> "" then
			for i = 2 to T_OTOSP1T.GetRecCount()
				if trim(T_OTOSP1T.GetFieldValue("TARIH" & j,i)) = "" then
					OncekiTarih = T_OTOSP1T.GetFieldValue("TARIH" & j,i-1)
					OncekiAttrib = T_OTOSP1T.GetFieldValue("ATTRIB" & j,i-1)				
					res = Doc.SetFieldValue("OTOSP1T","TARIH" & j,i,OncekiTarih)	
					res = Doc.SetFieldValue("OTOSP1T","ATTRIB" & j,i,OncekiAttrib)	
				end if
			Next
		end if
	Next
End Sub
Sub ToplamHesapla()
	Dim i,GunlukToplam, HaftalikToplam, AylikToplam, j, Ozellik, Deger
	For i = 1 to T_OTOSP1T.GetRecCount()
		GunlukToplam = 0
		HaftalikToplam = 0
		AylikToplam = 0
		For j = 1 to 40
			Ozellik = T_OTOSP1T.GetFieldValue("ATTRIB" & j,i)
			Deger = T_OTOSP1T.GetFieldValue("REPCOL" & j,i)
			if Ozellik = "G" then
				GunlukToplam = GunlukToplam + Deger
			Elseif Ozellik = "H" then
				HaftalikToplam = HaftalikToplam + Deger
			Elseif Ozellik = "A" then
				AylikToplam = AylikToplam + Deger			
			end if
		Next
		T_OTOSP1T.GUN_TOPLAMI(i) = GunlukToplam
		T_OTOSP1T.HAFTA_TOPLAMI(i) = HaftalikToplam
		T_OTOSP1T.AY_TOPLAMI(i) = AylikToplam
		T_OTOSP1T.GENEL_TOPLAM(i) = GunlukToplam + HaftalikToplam + AylikToplam 
	Next
End Sub
Function Temizle(Yazi)
	dim i
  For i = 1 To 4
    Yazi = Replace(Yazi, "  ", " ")
  Next
  Temizle = Yazi
End Function
Function FmtTarih(Tarih)
	dim Yil, Ay, Gun
	Yil = Left(Tarih,4)
	Ay = Mid(Tarih,5,2)
	Gun = Right(Tarih,2)
'	FmtTarih = Gun & "/" & Ay & "/" & Yil
	FmtTarih = Yil & "/" & Ay & "/" & Gun
End Function
Function Pazartesi(Tarih)
	dim d7Date, k, Gun
	Set d7Date = Doc.CreateSObject("d7Date")
	k = d7Date.SetDate(Tarih)
	Gun = d7Date.GetDOW
	Pazartesi = Doc.StrDate_Increment(d7Date.GetNorDate, (-1 * Gun) + 1)
End Function
Function ElemanSayisi(objXMLDoc)
	Dim Root, NodeList, i, Attr, Schedule, Sc, yy, ScCn
	Set Root = objXMLDoc.documentElement
	Set NodeList = Root.getElementsByTagName("PART")
	For i = 0 To NodeList.Length - 1
		Set Attr = NodeList.Item(i).Attributes
		Set Schedule = NodeList.Item(i).getElementsByTagName("SCHEDULE_ITEM")
		For Each Sc In Schedule
			Set ScCn = Sc.childNodes
			If ScCn.Item(1).Text <> "0" Or _
					Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","AD",Doc.LeftJustify(Temizle(Attr.Item(0).Text),24),"") <> "" Then
				yy = yy + 1 
			End If
		Next
	Next
	ElemanSayisi = yy
End Function
Function ElemanSayisiHafta(objXMLDoc)

	Dim Root, NodeList, i, Attr, Schedule, Sc, yy, Qty, ScCn, ForecastType
	Set Root = objXMLDoc.documentElement
	Set NodeList = Root.getElementsByTagName("PART")
	For i = 0 To NodeList.Length - 1
		Set Attr = NodeList.Item(i).Attributes
		Set Schedule = NodeList.Item(i).getElementsByTagName("FORECAST_ITEM")
		For Each Sc In Schedule
			Set Qty = Sc.SelectSingleNode("NET_QUANTITY")
			ForecastType = Sc.Attributes.Item(0).Text
			Set ScCn = Sc.childNodes
'			If Qty.Text <> "0" and ForecastType = "W" then
			If Qty.Text <> "0" then
				yy = yy + 1 
			End If
		Next
	Next
	ElemanSayisiHafta = yy
End Function
Function ElemanSayisiAy(objXMLDoc)
	Dim Root, NodeList, i, Attr, Schedule, Sc, yy, Qty, ScCn, ForecastType

	Set Root = objXMLDoc.documentElement
	Set NodeList = Root.getElementsByTagName("PART")
	For i = 0 To NodeList.Length - 1
		Set Attr = NodeList.Item(i).Attributes
		Set Schedule = NodeList.Item(i).getElementsByTagName("FORECAST_ITEM")
		For Each Sc In Schedule
			Set Qty = Sc.SelectSingleNode("NET_QUANTITY")
			ForecastType = Sc.Attributes.Item(0).Text
			Set ScCn = Sc.childNodes
			If Qty.Text <> "0" and ForecastType = "F" then
				yy = yy + 1 
			End If
		Next
	Next
	ElemanSayisiAy = yy
End Function
Function AlanBul1(ArananTarih,ArananAttr)
	dim i,Attr,Tarih
	AlanBul1=" "
	if ArananAttr = "W" then
		ArananAttr = "H"
	Elseif ArananAttr = "F" then
		ArananAttr = "A"
	Elseif ArananAttr = "H" then
		ArananAttr = "H"
	Elseif ArananAttr = "A" then
		ArananAttr = "A"
	Elseif ArananAttr = "G" then
		ArananAttr = "G"
	end if
	for i = 1 to 40
		Tarih = T_OTOSP1T.GetFieldValue("TARIH" & i,1)
		Attr = T_OTOSP1T.GetFieldValue("ATTRIB" & i,1)
		if trim(Tarih) = trim(ArananTarih) and trim(Attr) = trim(ArananAttr) then
			AlanBul1 = "REPCOL" & i
			Exit Function
		end if
	Next
End Function
Function IlkTarih(Table)
	Dim i
	Table.Sort("RTESTARIH")
	For i = 1 To Table.GetRecCount()
		If Table.SF_MIKTAR(i) > 0 Then
			IlkTarih = Table.RTESTARIH(i)
			Exit Function
		End If
	Next
End Function
Function SonTarih(Table)
	Dim i
	Table.Sort("<RTESTARIH")
	For i = 1 to Table.GetRecCount() 
		If Table.SF_MIKTAR(i) > 0 Then
			SonTarih = Table.RTESTARIH(i)
			Exit Function
		End If
	Next
End Function
Sub ExceleYaz(Table)
	Dim Excel, WB, i, k

	'//1.8.2012
	'//Doc.StatusBarMessage "Excel'e yazýlýyor"
	Doc.StatusBarMessage Doc.GetMyMessage(4110)

	Set Excel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
	Set WB = Excel.Workbooks.Add
	'//2.8.2012
	'//Wb.Sheets(1).cells(1,1) = "Stok Kodu"
	'//Wb.Sheets(1).cells(1,2) = "Miktar"
	'//Wb.Sheets(1).cells(1,3) = "Tarih"
	'//Wb.Sheets(1).cells(1,4) = "Program No"
	'//Wb.Sheets(1).cells(1,5) = "Fabrika Kodu"
	'//Wb.Sheets(1).cells(1,6) = "Sevk Edilen Fabrika"
	'//Wb.Sheets(1).cells(1,7) = "Ara Sevk"
	'//Wb.Sheets(1).cells(1,8) = "Lokasyon"
	Wb.Sheets(1).cells(1,1) = Doc.GetMyMessage(1051)
	Wb.Sheets(1).cells(1,2) = Doc.GetMyMessage(3137)
	Wb.Sheets(1).cells(1,3) = Doc.GetMyMessage(1244)
	Wb.Sheets(1).cells(1,4) = Doc.GetMyMessage(4140)
	Wb.Sheets(1).cells(1,5) = Doc.GetMyMessage(4141)
	Wb.Sheets(1).cells(1,6) = Doc.GetMyMessage(4142)
	Wb.Sheets(1).cells(1,7) = Doc.GetMyMessage(4143)
	Wb.Sheets(1).cells(1,8) = Doc.GetMyMessage(3953)

	for i = 1 to table.GetRecCount
		Doc.StatusBarMessage i & " / " & table.GetRecCount
		Wb.Sheets(1).cells(i+1,1) = TABLE.KOD(i)
		Wb.Sheets(1).cells(i+1,2) = TABLE.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		Wb.Sheets(1).cells(i+1,3) = TABLE.RTESTARIH(i)	
		Wb.Sheets(1).cells(i+1,4) = TABLE.PROGRAM_NO(i)
		Wb.Sheets(1).cells(i+1,5) = TABLE.FABRIKA_KODU(i)
		Wb.Sheets(1).cells(i+1,6) = TABLE.SEVK_EDILEN_FB(i)
		Wb.Sheets(1).cells(i+1,7) = TABLE.ARA_SEVK(i)
		Wb.Sheets(1).cells(i+1,8) = TABLE.LOKASYON(i)
	Next
	excel.visible = true
End Sub
Function satirBul(satirNo,ST40)
	dim CHKodu, i, AraStr, BulStr, StokKodu
	satirBul = 0
	if trim(Doc.GetDBFieldvalue("STOK00","KOD",ST40.KOD(satirNo),"")) <> "" then
		StokKodu = ST40.KOD(satirNo)
	else
		CHKodu = T_OTOSP1E.CH_KODU
		StokKodu = Doc.Get0G0U(ST40.KOD(satirNo),CHKodu,"12","")
	end if

	For i = 1 to T_OTOSP1T.GetRecCount()
		AraStr = trim(StokKodu) + trim(ST40.PROGRAM_NO(satirNo)) + trim(ST40.FABRIKA_KODU(satirNo)) + _
						trim(ST40.SEVK_EDILEN_FB(satirNo)) + trim(ST40.ARA_SEVK(satirNo))
		BulStr = trim(T_OTOSP1T.KOD(i)) + trim(T_OTOSP1T.PROGRAM_NO(i)) + trim(T_OTOSP1T.FABRIKA_KODU(i)) + _
						trim(T_OTOSP1T.SEVK_EDILEN_FB(i)) + trim(T_OTOSP1T.ARA_SEVK(i))
		if trim(BulStr) = trim(AraStr) then
			satirBul = i
			exit Function
		end if
	Next
End Function
Function GecersizBul(StokKodu)
	GecersizBul = 0
	dim x
	for each x in GecersizKodlar
		if x = StokKodu then
			GecersizBul = 1
			Exit Function
		end if
	Next
End Function
Sub VPEYaz(HataArray, aciklama)
	dim x,res
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(ustMargin,solMargin,5,sagMargin-solmargin,aciklama)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	for each x in HataArray
		if (pe1.getTop + satirYuksekligi) > altMargin then
			res = pe1.PageBreak()
			satirPozr = 25
		else
			satirPozr = pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi
		end if
		res = pe1.Write(satirPozr,solMargin + 5,5,sagMargin-solmargin - 5,x)
	Next
	UstMargin=satirPozr
End Sub
Sub vpe_ayarlari()
	Dim res
	ustMargin = 25
	solMargin = 15 
	sagMargin = 200
	AltMargin = 275
	satirYuksekligi = 6
	set pe1 = Doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(0,"Hata", -1,-1,"")
End Sub
Sub vpe_son()
	Dim res
	res = pe1.Show()
	doc.ActivateAftermacro(1)
End Sub
Function GetIrsNo(IrsaliyeNo)
	IrsaliyeNo = Cstr(IrsaliyeNo)
	Dim rIrsNo, Bosluk
	if trim(IrsaliyeNo) = "" Then 
		GetIrsNo = IrsaliyeNo
		Exit Function
	end if
	rIrsNo = StrReverse(trim(IrsaliyeNo))
	Bosluk = Instr(1, rIrsNo, " ")
	if Bosluk > 0 Then
		GetIrsNo = StrReverse(Left(rIrsNo,Bosluk - 1))
	Else
		GetIrsNo = StrReverse(rIrsNo)
	End if
End Function
Function FOSNConnect(KullaniciAdi, KullaniciSifre)
    Dim urlx, objXMLHTTP
    urlx = "https://login.ford.com.tr/authenticate4OutPrg.aspx?userid=" & KullaniciAdi & "&password=" & KullaniciSifre

    Set objXMLHTTP = CreateObject("Microsoft.XMLHTTP")
    objXMLHTTP.Open "POST", urlx, False
    objXMLHTTP.Send
    While objXMLHTTP.readyState <> 4
        objXMLHTTP.waitForResponse 1000
    Wend
    
    FOSNConnect = objXMLHTTP.getAllResponseHeaders
End Function
Function GetPrgXML(cookieStr, GunlukHaftalik)
    Dim sTargetDataPage, sRef, objXMLHTTP, j

	if GunlukHaftalik = "G" Then
		sTargetDataPage = "https://fosnapp.ford.com.tr/Page_prg_daily_summary.aspx?CMD=XML"
	Elseif GunlukHaftalik = "H" Then
		sTargetDataPage = "https://fosnapp.ford.com.tr/Page_prg_weekly_summary.aspx?CMD=XML"
	End if
    Set objXMLHTTP = CreateObject("Microsoft.XMLHTTP")
    objXMLHTTP.Open "POST", sTargetDataPage, False
    objXMLHTTP.setRequestHeader "cookie", "x=y"
    objXMLHTTP.setRequestHeader "Cookie", cookieStr

    On Error Resume Next
    objXMLHTTP.Send
    If Err.Number = -2147024891 Then
        Err.Clear
        objXMLHTTP.Open "POST", sTargetDataPage, False
        objXMLHTTP.setRequestHeader "cookie", "x=y"
        objXMLHTTP.setRequestHeader "Cookie", cookieStr
        objXMLHTTP.Send
    ElseIf Err.Number > 0 Then
        Set GetGunlukPrg = Nothing
        Exit Function
    End If
    While objXMLHTTP.readyState <> 4
        objXMLHTTP.waitForResponse 1000
    Wend
    
    Set GetPrgXML = objXMLHTTP.responseXML
    Set objXMLHTTP = Nothing
End Function
Function FOSNLogOut()
    Dim urlx, objXMLHTTP
    urlx = "https://login.ford.com.tr/logout.aspx"
    Set objXMLHTTP = CreateObject("Microsoft.XMLHTTP")
    objXMLHTTP.Open "POST", urlx, False
    objXMLHTTP.Send
    While objXMLHTTP.readyState <> 4
        objXMLHTTP.waitForResponse 1000
    Wend
    Set objXMLHTTP = Nothing
End Function

Sub Makro1()
	Dim MTable
	Set MTable=Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLE=STACK","IO")
	MTable.Addfield2 "AD1","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "AD2","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "AD3","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "AD4","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "TUMSTOK", "CHAR", 1, 0, "L", "", ""
	MTable.Addfield2 "SATINALINAN", "CHAR", 1, 0, "L", "", ""

	Dim MTable2

	MTable2.Addfield2 "AD","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTable2.Addfield2 "GK_1","CHAR",10,0,"L","",""
	MTable2.Addfield2 "GK_2","CHAR",10,0,"L","",""
	MTable2.Addfield2 "GK_3","CHAR",10,0,"L","",""
	MTable2.Addfield2 "GK_4","CHAR",10,0,"L","",""
	MTable2.Addfield2 "GK_5","CHAR",10,0,"L","",""
	MTable2.Addfield2 "GK_6","CHAR",10,0,"L","",""
	MTable2.Addfield2 "GK_7","CHAR",10,0,"L","",""
	MTable2.Addfield2 "GK_8","CHAR",10,0,"L","",""
	MTable2.Addfield2 "GK_9","CHAR",10,0,"L","",""
	MTable2.Addfield2 "GK_10","CHAR",10,0,"L","",""

	MTable.SATINALINAN = "E"

	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	Doc.LoadSubForm_Dyn "MAKROFORMU2",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par
End Sub

Sub Suz()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set MTable2 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE2")
	MTable2.SetRowAID 1,1,1
	MTable2.Empty
	
	If MTable.SATINALINAN = "E" Then 
		Kriter1 = "KART_TIPI = 'S'"
	Else
		Kriter1 = "KART_TIPI <> ' '"
	End If

	QStr = "SELECT *"  & _
				" FROM STOK00"  & _
				" WHERE 1=1 AND " & Kriter1
	if trim(MTable.AD1) <> "" Then
		QStr = QStr & " AND UPPER(STOK00.AD) LIKE '%" & UCase(trim(MTable.AD1)) & "%'"
	End if
	if trim(MTable.AD2) <> "" Then
		QStr = QStr & " AND UPPER(STOK00.AD) LIKE '%" & UCase(trim(MTable.AD2)) & "%'"
	End if
	if trim(MTable.AD3) <> "" Then
		QStr = QStr & " AND UPPER(STOK00.AD) LIKE '%" & UCase(trim(MTable.AD3)) & "%'"
	End if
	if trim(MTable.AD4) <> "" Then
		QStr = QStr & " AND UPPER(STOK00.AD) LIKE '%" & UCase(trim(MTable.AD4)) & "%'"
	End if

	Doc.RunSQLQuery "XXXX_1", QStr
	Set S00 = Doc.GetTableobject("XXXX_1")
	For i = 1 to S00.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Listeye Ekleniyor : " & i & " / " & S00.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = MTable2.AddRow()
		MTable2.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		MTable2.KOD = S00.KOD(i)
		MTable2.AD = S00.AD(i)
		MTable2.GK_1 = S00.GK_1(i)
		MTable2.GK_2 = S00.GK_2(i)
		MTable2.GK_3 = S00.GK_3(i)
		MTable2.GK_4 = S00.GK_4(i)
		MTable2.GK_5 = S00.GK_5(i)
		MTable2.GK_6 = S00.GK_6(i)
		MTable2.GK_7 = S00.GK_7(i)
		MTable2.GK_8 = S00.GK_8(i)
		MTable2.GK_9 = S00.GK_9(i)
		MTable2.GK_10 = S00.GK_10(i)
	Next
	MTable2.SetRowAID 0,0,0
	MTable2.SetCurrentRow(1)
End Sub

Sub Sec()
	Set MTable2 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE2")
	StokKodu = MTable2.KOD
	Doc.RUN_APP_CANCEL
	Set T1 = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK47T")
	T1.KOD = StokKodu
End Sub
